# ── POST /auth/login ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/auth/login", response_model=LoginResponse)
def login(body: LoginRequest, request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    Admin login. Looks up the username, verifies its bcrypt password, and
    returns an expiring signed token sent by the frontend as a Bearer header.
    """
    username = (body.username or "").strip()
    if not username or not body.password:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Username and password are required.")
    if not _login_allowed(request, username):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail="Too many login attempts. Try again in one minute.")

    user = db.query(AdminUser).filter(AdminUser.username == username).first()

    # Constant-time-ish comparison; generic error message either way.
    if not user or not verify_password(body.password, user.password):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid username or password.")

    token = create_access_token(user.username)

    logger.info(f"Admin login successful: {username}")
    return LoginResponse(token=token, username=user.username)


# ── POST /upload ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@app.post("/upload", response_model=UploadResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED)
async def upload_file(
    file: UploadFile = File(...),
    db:   Session    = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Accept an Excel/CSV upload and return IMMEDIATELY.

    Heavy work (parse + LLM column-mapping + dedup + bulk insert) runs in a
    background Celery task, so the request never times out on large files.
    The frontend polls GET /jobs/{job_id}/status:
        importing → queued (ready for ICP)  |  import_failed (see import_error)
    """
    # ── Validate extension ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    suffix = _file_suffix(file.filename)
    if suffix not in SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=f"Unsupported file type '{suffix}'. Allowed: {SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS}",
        )

    # ── Read + size-check ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    max_bytes = settings.MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB * 1024 * 1024
    raw_bytes = await file.read(max_bytes + 1)
    size_mb   = len(raw_bytes) / (1024 * 1024)
    if size_mb > settings.MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=413,
            detail=f"File too large ({size_mb:.1f} MB). Max: {settings.MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB} MB",
        )

    # ── Create the job in 'importing' state ───────────────────────────────────
    job = Job(
        status=    "importing",
        total_rows= None,
        file_name=  file.filename,
        file_type=  suffix.lstrip("."),
    )
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()                      # get job.id
    job_id = str(job.id)

    # ── Store the raw file to Blob so the worker can read it ──────────────────
    blob_name = f"uploads/{job_id}/source{suffix}"
    if not await run_in_threadpool(_store_raw_to_blob, raw_bytes, blob_name):
        job.status = "import_failed"
        job.import_error = "Could not store the uploaded file. Check Blob storage."
        db.commit()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="File storage unavailable — try again.")

    # ── Commit the job BEFORE dispatching (so the worker can find it) ─────────
    db.commit()

    # ── Dispatch the background import ────────────────────────────────────────
    from app.tasks.import_task import import_file
    import_file.delay(job_id, blob_name, suffix, file.filename)

    logger.info(f"Job {job_id} accepted for import: '{file.filename}'")

    return UploadResponse(
        job_id=    job_id,
        file_name= file.filename,
        file_type= suffix.lstrip("."),
        status=    "importing",
        message=   "File received. Import is running — poll /jobs/{job_id}/status.",
    )

# ── POST /jobs/{job_id}/icp ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.post("/jobs/{job_id}/icp", status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED)
def submit_icp(
    job_id:  str,
    body:    ICPRequest,
    db:      Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Attach an ICP prompt to a job and dispatch all Celery tasks.
    Returns immediately — frontend polls /jobs/{job_id}/status.
    """
    job: Job = _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)

    if job.status not in ("queued",):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail=f"Job is already '{job.status}'. Only 'queued' jobs can be started.",
        )

    if not body.icp_prompt.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="icp_prompt cannot be empty.")

    # Save ICP prompt and mark running
    job.icp_prompt = body.icp_prompt.strip()
    job.status     = "running"
    db.flush()

    # Load all lead IDs for this job
    lead_ids = [str(r[0]) for r in db.query(Lead.id).filter(Lead.job_id == job_id).all()]

    if not lead_ids:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Job has no leads to process.")

    # Commit before dispatch so workers can always observe the running state.
    db.commit()
    try:
        header = [process_lead.s(lid, job.icp_prompt) for lid in lead_ids]
        callback = finalise_job.s(job_id)
        chord(header)(callback)
    except Exception as exc:
        job.status = "queued"
        db.commit()
        logger.exception("Failed to dispatch job %s", job_id)
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Processing queue unavailable. Please retry.") from exc

    logger.info(f"Job {job_id}: dispatched {len(lead_ids)} tasks")

    return {
        "job_id":      job_id,
        "status":      "running",
        "tasks_queued": len(lead_ids),
        "message":     f"Processing {len(lead_ids)} leads. Poll /jobs/{job_id}/status for progress.",
    }


# ── GET /jobs ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/jobs")
def list_jobs(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """List all jobs, newest first."""
    jobs = db.query(Job).order_by(Job.created_at.desc()).limit(100).all()
    return [_job_to_status(j) for j in jobs]


@app.get("/dashboard/stats")
def dashboard_stats(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Return compact aggregates without transferring every lead to the UI."""
    job_row = db.query(
        func.count(Job.id).label("total"),
        func.sum(case((Job.status == "completed", 1), else_=0)).label("completed"),
        func.sum(case((Job.status.in_(("importing", "queued", "running")), 1), else_=0)).label("active"),
        func.sum(case((Job.status.in_(("failed", "import_failed")), 1), else_=0)).label("failed"),
    ).one()
    lead_row = (
        db.query(
            func.count(FinalScore.id).label("total"),
            func.sum(case((FinalScore.priority == "High", 1), else_=0)).label("high"),
            func.sum(case((FinalScore.priority == "Medium", 1), else_=0)).label("medium"),
            func.sum(case((FinalScore.priority == "Low", 1), else_=0)).label("low"),
            func.avg(FinalScore.total_score).label("average"),
        )
        .join(Lead, Lead.id == FinalScore.lead_id)
        .join(Job, Job.id == Lead.job_id)
        .filter(Job.status == "completed")
        .one()
    )
    return {
        "total_jobs": int(job_row.total or 0),
        "completed_jobs": int(job_row.completed or 0),
        "active_jobs": int(job_row.active or 0),
        "failed_jobs": int(job_row.failed or 0),
        "total_leads": int(lead_row.total or 0),
        "high_priority": int(lead_row.high or 0),
        "medium_priority": int(lead_row.medium or 0),
        "low_priority": int(lead_row.low or 0),
        "avg_final_score": round(float(lead_row.average or 0), 1),
    }


# ── GET /jobs/{job_id}/status ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/jobs/{job_id}/status", response_model=JobStatusResponse)
def job_status(job_id: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    job = _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)
    try:
        db.refresh(job)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"db.refresh failed for job {job_id}: {e} — using cached state")
    return _job_to_status(job)


# ── GET /jobs/{job_id}/results ────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/jobs/{job_id}/results", response_model=PaginatedResults)
def job_results(
    job_id: str,
    limit:  int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500, description="Page size (max 500)."),
    offset: int = Query(0,  ge=0,          description="Rows to skip (page offset)."),
    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=100),
    priority: Optional[Literal["High", "Medium", "Low"]] = None,
    sort_by: Literal["row_index", "company_name", "contact_name", "email_address", "final_score", "priority"] = "row_index",
    sort_dir: Literal["asc", "desc"] = "asc",
    db:     Session = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Return processed lead results for a job — PAGINATED.

    Performance: uses joinedload(final_score) + selectinload(validation_results)
    to eager-load relationships in ~2 queries instead of the old N+1 explosion
    (which fired ~1,000 queries for 500 leads and made the page hang).

    Response is a PaginatedResults wrapper:
        { items: [...], total, limit, offset, returned, has_more }
    """
    _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)

    base = db.query(Lead).outerjoin(FinalScore, FinalScore.lead_id == Lead.id).filter(Lead.job_id == job_id)
    if search and search.strip():
        escaped = search.strip().replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("%", "\\%").replace("_", "\\_")
        term = f"%{escaped}%"
        base = base.filter(or_(
            Lead.company_name.ilike(term, escape="\\"), Lead.contact_name.ilike(term, escape="\\"),
            Lead.email_address.ilike(term, escape="\\"),
        ))
    if priority:
        base = base.filter(FinalScore.priority == priority)

    # Total count for the whole job (cheap COUNT query, no row hydration).
    total = base.with_entities(func.count(Lead.id)).scalar() or 0

    sort_columns = {
        "row_index": Lead.row_index,
        "company_name": Lead.company_name,
        "contact_name": Lead.contact_name,
        "email_address": Lead.email_address,
        "final_score": FinalScore.total_score,
        "priority": FinalScore.priority,
    }
    order_column = sort_columns[sort_by]
    order = order_column.desc() if sort_dir == "desc" else order_column.asc()
    leads = (
        base
        .options(
            joinedload(Lead.final_score),           # 1-to-1 → JOIN in the same query
            selectinload(Lead.validation_results),  # 1-to-many → one extra IN() query
        )
        .order_by(order, Lead.row_index.asc())
        .offset(offset)
        .limit(limit)
        .all()
    )

    items = [_lead_to_response(lead) for lead in leads]

    return PaginatedResults(
        items=    items,
        total=    int(total),
        limit=    limit,
        offset=   offset,
        returned= len(items),
        has_more= (offset + len(items)) < int(total),
    )


# ── GET /jobs/{job_id}/results/{lead_id} ──────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/jobs/{job_id}/results/{lead_id}", response_model=LeadResultResponse)
def lead_result(job_id: str, lead_id: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Return a single lead result."""
    _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)
    lead = (
        db.query(Lead)
        .filter(Lead.id == lead_id, Lead.job_id == job_id)
        .options(
            joinedload(Lead.final_score),
            selectinload(Lead.validation_results),
        )
        .first()
    )
    if not lead:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Lead not found.")
    return _lead_to_response(lead)


# ── GET /jobs/{job_id}/export/excel ──────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/jobs/{job_id}/export/excel")
def export_excel(job_id: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Build and STREAM the enriched .xlsx (no blob redirect → no CORS issues)."""
    job = _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)
    _require_completed(job)

    data = build_excel_bytes(job_id, db)
    return StreamingResponse(
        io.BytesIO(data),
        media_type="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="job_{job_id}.xlsx"'},
    )


# ── GET /jobs/{job_id}/export/csv ─────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/jobs/{job_id}/export/csv")
def export_csv(job_id: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Build and STREAM the enriched .csv (no blob redirect → no CORS issues)."""
    job = _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)
    _require_completed(job)

    data = build_csv_bytes(job_id, db)
    return StreamingResponse(
        io.BytesIO(data),
        media_type="text/csv",
        headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="job_{job_id}.csv"'},
    )


# ── DELETE /jobs/{job_id} ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.delete("/jobs/{job_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def delete_job(job_id: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Delete a job and all associated data (cascade + Qdrant vectors)."""
    job = _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)

    # Remove this session's vectors from Qdrant (best-effort — never block delete).
    try:
        qdrant_delete_job(job_id)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to delete Qdrant vectors for job {job_id}: {e}")
    _delete_job_blobs(job_id)

    db.delete(job)
    logger.info(f"Job {job_id} deleted")

@app.get("/jobs/{job_id}/token-report")
def token_report(job_id: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    """Per-stage token consumption breakdown for a job."""
    from sqlalchemy import func as sqlfunc
    from app.models import LLMCallLog
    _get_job_or_404(job_id, db)

    rows = (
        db.query(
            LLMCallLog.stage,
            LLMCallLog.module,
            sqlfunc.sum(LLMCallLog.prompt_tokens).label("prompt"),
            sqlfunc.sum(LLMCallLog.completion_tokens).label("completion"),
            sqlfunc.sum(LLMCallLog.total_tokens).label("total"),
            sqlfunc.count(LLMCallLog.id).label("calls"),
            sqlfunc.avg(LLMCallLog.duration_ms).label("avg_ms"),
        )
        .filter(LLMCallLog.job_id == job_id)
        .group_by(LLMCallLog.stage, LLMCallLog.module)
        .order_by(sqlfunc.sum(LLMCallLog.total_tokens).desc())
        .all()
    )

    total_tokens = sum(r.total or 0 for r in rows)
    return {
        "job_id": job_id,
        "total_tokens": total_tokens,
        "by_stage": [
            {
                "stage":      r.stage,
                "module":     r.module,
                "calls":      r.calls,
                "prompt":     r.prompt,
                "completion": r.completion,
                "total":      r.total,
                "pct":        round((r.total or 0) / total_tokens * 100, 1) if total_tokens else 0,
                "avg_ms":     int(r.avg_ms or 0),
            }
            for r in rows
        ],
    }


# ── GET /health ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@app.get("/health")
def health():
    db_ok = ping_db()
    return {
        "status":   "ok" if db_ok else "degraded",
        "database": "ok" if db_ok else "unreachable",
        "env":      settings.APP_ENV,
    }


@app.get("/health/live")
def liveness():
    """Cheap App Service liveness probe; does not depend on external services."""
    return {"status": "ok"}


@app.get("/health/ready")
def readiness():
    """Dependency-aware readiness probe for deployment slots and rollouts."""
    redis_ok = False
    try:
        import redis
        redis_ok = bool(redis.Redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL).ping())
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("Redis readiness check failed")
    db_ok = ping_db()
    if not (db_ok and redis_ok):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail={"database": db_ok, "redis": redis_ok})
    return {"status": "ready", "database": "ok", "redis": "ok"}


# ── Internal helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _get_job_or_404(job_id: str, db: Session) -> Job:
    job = db.query(Job).filter(Job.id == job_id).first()
    if not job:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Job '{job_id}' not found.")
    return job


def _require_completed(job: Job) -> None:
    if job.status != "completed":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail=f"Job is '{job.status}'. Exports are only available for completed jobs.",
        )


def _job_to_status(job: Job) -> JobStatusResponse:
    return JobStatusResponse(
        job_id=             str(job.id),
        status=             job.status,
        file_name=          job.file_name,
        file_type=          job.file_type,
        total_rows=         job.total_rows,
        processed_rows=     job.processed_rows,
        failed_rows=        getattr(job, "failed_rows", 0) or 0,
        progress_pct=       job.progress_pct,
        duplicates_removed= getattr(job, "duplicates_removed", None),
        import_error=       getattr(job, "import_error", None),
        created_at=         _to_ist(job.created_at),        # ← convert
        completed_at=       _to_ist(job.completed_at),      # ← convert
        llm_model=          getattr(job, "llm_model", None),
        embed_model=        getattr(job, "embed_model", None),
        total_tokens_used=  getattr(job, "total_tokens_used", None),
        prompt_tokens=      getattr(job, "prompt_tokens", None),
        completion_tokens=  getattr(job, "completion_tokens", None),
    )


def _lead_to_response(lead: Lead) -> LeadResultResponse:
    fs:   Optional[FinalScore]   = lead.final_score
    vrs:  list[ValidationResult] = lead.validation_results or []

    contact_vr = next((v for v in vrs if v.stage == "contact"), None)
    web_vr     = next((v for v in vrs if v.stage == "web"), None)

    source_urls: list[str] = []
    if web_vr and web_vr.sources_json:
        try:
            source_urls = json.loads(web_vr.sources_json)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return LeadResultResponse(
        lead_id=        str(lead.id),
        contact_name=   lead.contact_name,
        company_name=   lead.company_name,
        job_title=      lead.job_title,
        email_address=  lead.email_address,
        final_score=    fs.total_score    if fs else None,
        priority=       fs.priority       if fs else None,
        reasoning=      fs.reasoning      if fs else None,
        growth_signals= fs.growth_signals if fs else None,
        outreach_intelligence=fs.outreach_intelligence if fs else None,
        icp_score=      fs.icp_score      if fs else None,
        authority_score=fs.authority_score if fs else None,
        data_score=     fs.data_score     if fs else None,
        growth_score=   fs.growth_score   if fs else None,
        contact_score=  fs.contact_score  if fs else None,
        validation_status=("Passed" if contact_vr.passed else "Failed") if contact_vr else "Pending",
        source_urls=    source_urls,
        company_description=lead.company_description,
        technologies_used=  lead.technologies_used,
    )


def _file_suffix(filename: Optional[str]) -> str:
    if not filename:
        return ""
    return "." + filename.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower() if "." in filename else ""


def _upload_source_file(raw: bytes, job_id: str, filename: str, suffix: str) -> None:
    """Store the original uploaded file in Azure Blob (best-effort)."""
    try:
        client    = BlobServiceClient.from_connection_string(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONNECTION_STRING)
        container = client.get_container_client(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONTAINER)
        blob_name = f"uploads/{job_id}/source{suffix}"
        container.get_blob_client(blob_name).upload_blob(raw, overwrite=True)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Failed to upload source file for job {job_id}: {e}")

def _store_raw_to_blob(raw: bytes, blob_name: str) -> bool:
    """Upload raw bytes to Blob at the given name. Returns True on success."""
    try:
        client = BlobServiceClient.from_connection_string(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONNECTION_STRING)
        container = client.get_container_client(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONTAINER)
        try:
            container.get_container_properties()
        except Exception:
            try:
                container.create_container()
            except Exception:
                pass
        container.get_blob_client(blob_name).upload_blob(raw, overwrite=True)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"_store_raw_to_blob failed for '{blob_name}': {e}")
        return False


def _delete_job_blobs(job_id: str) -> None:
    """Best-effort cleanup of source, scrape, and export objects for a job."""
    try:
        client = BlobServiceClient.from_connection_string(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONNECTION_STRING)
        container = client.get_container_client(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONTAINER)
        for prefix in (f"uploads/{job_id}/", f"scraped/{job_id}/", f"exports/{job_id}/"):
            names = [blob.name for blob in container.list_blobs(name_starts_with=prefix)]
            for start in range(0, len(names), 256):
                container.delete_blobs(*names[start:start + 256])
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Blob cleanup failed for job %s: %s", job_id, exc)


def _login_allowed(request: Request, username: str) -> bool:
    """Redis-backed 10-attempt/minute login throttle; fail open on cache outage."""
    try:
        import hashlib
        import redis
        ip = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
        digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{ip}|{username.lower()}".encode()).hexdigest()
        client = redis.Redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL)
        key = f"ql:login:{digest}"
        attempts = client.incr(key)
        if attempts == 1:
            client.expire(key, 60)
        return attempts <= 10
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Login throttle unavailable: %s", exc)
        return True

IST = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))

def _to_ist(dt):
    """Convert a naive/UTC datetime to IST for API responses."""
    if dt is None:
        return None
    # DB datetimes are UTC but usually 'naive' (no tzinfo) — attach UTC first.
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return dt.astimezone(IST)

"""
models.py

SQLAlchemy ORM models — one class per Azure SQL table.
Mirrors the schema exactly. Import Base from database.py.

Import pattern everywhere else:
    from app.models import Job, Lead, ValidationResult, FinalScore, ProcessingLog

import uuid

    Boolean, DateTime, Float, ForeignKey,
    Integer, String, Text, func,
)

def _new_uuid() -> str:
    return str(uuid.uuid4())


# ── jobs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
class Job(Base):
    __tablename__ = "jobs"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        UNIQUEIDENTIFIER, primary_key=True, default=_new_uuid
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), nullable=False, default="queued")
    # queued | running | completed | failed

    total_rows: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    processed_rows: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    failed_rows: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)

    icp_prompt: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    file_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=True)
    file_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=True)
    duplicates_removed: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    import_error:       Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    # xlsx | csv

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime, nullable=False, server_default=func.getutcdate()
    )
    completed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    # ── Add to Job model (inside the class, after existing mapped_column lines) ──
    llm_model:      Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)
    embed_model:    Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)
    total_tokens_used: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    prompt_tokens:  Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    completion_tokens: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)

    # Relationships
    leads: Mapped[list["Lead"]] = relationship(
        "Lead", back_populates="job", cascade="all, delete-orphan"
    )
    logs: Mapped[list["ProcessingLog"]] = relationship(
        "ProcessingLog", back_populates="job", cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        foreign_keys="ProcessingLog.job_id",
    )

    llm_call_logs: Mapped[list["LLMCallLog"]] = relationship(
        "LLMCallLog", cascade="all, delete-orphan",
        foreign_keys="LLMCallLog.job_id",
    )    

    @property
    def progress_pct(self) -> float:
        if not self.total_rows:
            return 0.0
        return round((self.processed_rows / self.total_rows) * 100, 1)

    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<Job id={self.id} status={self.status} rows={self.processed_rows}/{self.total_rows}>"

"""
parsers/_common.py
──────────────────
Shared cleaning logic for both the Excel and CSV parsers.

Previously `_clean` lived in excel_parser.py and csv_parser.py imported it from
there — an awkward coupling. It now lives here so both parsers depend on a
neutral shared module instead of each other.

Import pattern:
    from app.parsers._common import clean_dataframe
"""

import logging

import pandas as pd

from app.config import LEAD_SOURCE_DEFAULT, settings
from app.utils.column_mapper import map_columns, apply_mapping

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def clean_dataframe(raw_df: pd.DataFrame, source_label: str, job_id: str = None) -> pd.DataFrame:  # ← add
    if raw_df.empty:
        raise ValueError(f"{source_label} file is empty.")

    raw_df.columns = [str(c).strip() for c in raw_df.columns]
    raw_df = raw_df.dropna(how="all").reset_index(drop=True)

    if raw_df.empty:
        raise ValueError(f"{source_label} file has no data rows after dropping empties.")
    if len(raw_df) > settings.MAX_ROWS_PER_UPLOAD:
        raise ValueError(
            f"File contains {len(raw_df):,} rows; the configured limit is "
            f"{settings.MAX_ROWS_PER_UPLOAD:,}. Split the file or raise MAX_ROWS_PER_UPLOAD."
        )

    logger.info(f"[{source_label}] {len(raw_df)} rows, {len(raw_df.columns)} columns detected")

    mapping = map_columns(raw_df.columns.tolist(), sample_df=raw_df, job_id=job_id)  # ← pass it
    df = apply_mapping(raw_df, mapping)

    if "Lead Source" in df.columns:
        df["Lead Source"] = df["Lead Source"].replace("", LEAD_SOURCE_DEFAULT)
    else:
        df["Lead Source"] = LEAD_SOURCE_DEFAULT

    df = df.fillna("").astype(str)
    df = df.apply(lambda col: col.str.strip())
    df = df.replace({"nan": "", "None": "", "NaN": ""})

    logger.info(f"[{source_label}] Parsed → {len(df)} leads ready")
    return df

END FILE: backend/app/parsers/_common.py

"""
parsers/csv_parser.py
─────────────────────
Reads .csv files, auto-detects encoding + delimiter, and returns a clean
29-column DataFrame. Passes the raw DataFrame to column_mapper (via
_common.clean_dataframe) so the LLM can inspect sample cell values before
deciding the mapping.
"""

import io
import logging
from typing import Union

import chardet
import pandas as pd

# No longer imports _clean from excel_parser — uses the shared _common module.
from app.parsers._common import clean_dataframe

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_DELIMITER_CANDIDATES = [",", ";", "\t", "|"]


def parse_csv(source: Union[bytes, str, io.BytesIO], job_id: str = None) -> pd.DataFrame:  # ← add
    raw_bytes, encoding = _resolve_source(source)
    delimiter = _detect_delimiter(raw_bytes, encoding)
    logger.info(f"[CSV] Detected encoding={encoding}, delimiter={repr(delimiter)}")

    try:
        raw_df = pd.read_csv(
            io.BytesIO(raw_bytes),
            encoding=encoding,
            sep=delimiter,
            dtype=str,
            keep_default_na=False,
            on_bad_lines="warn",
        )
    except Exception as e:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to read CSV file: {e}") from e

    return clean_dataframe(raw_df, source_label="CSV", job_id=job_id)  # ← pass it


def _resolve_source(source: Union[bytes, str, io.BytesIO]) -> tuple[bytes, str]:
    if isinstance(source, str):
        with open(source, "rb") as f:
            raw_bytes = f.read()
    elif isinstance(source, io.BytesIO):
        raw_bytes = source.read()
        source.seek(0)
    elif isinstance(source, bytes):
        raw_bytes = source
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported source type: {type(source)}")

    detection = chardet.detect(raw_bytes[:50_000])
    encoding  = detection.get("encoding") or "utf-8"
    if encoding.lower() in ("ascii", "iso-8859-1", "latin-1"):
        encoding = "latin-1"

    return raw_bytes, encoding


def _detect_delimiter(raw_bytes: bytes, encoding: str) -> str:
    import csv
    try:
        sample  = raw_bytes[:4096].decode(encoding, errors="replace")
        dialect = csv.Sniffer().sniff(sample, delimiters="".join(_DELIMITER_CANDIDATES))
        return dialect.delimiter
    except csv.Error:
        logger.warning("[CSV] Delimiter sniffing failed — defaulting to ','")
        return ","

END FILE: backend/app/parsers/csv_parser.py
==========================================================================================

"""
parsers/excel_parser.py
───────────────────────
Reads .xlsx / .xls / .xlsm files and returns a clean 29-column DataFrame.
Passes the raw DataFrame to column_mapper (via _common.clean_dataframe) so the
LLM can inspect sample cell values — not just column names — before mapping.
"""

import io
import logging
from typing import Union

import pandas as pd

from app.parsers._common import clean_dataframe

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def parse_excel(
    source: Union[bytes, str, io.BytesIO],
    sheet_index: int = 0,
    job_id: str = None,       # ← add this
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if isinstance(source, bytes):
        source = io.BytesIO(source)

    try:
        raw_df = pd.read_excel(
            source,
            sheet_name=sheet_index,
            header=0,
            dtype=str,
            keep_default_na=False,
        )
    except Exception as e:
        raise ValueError(f"Failed to read Excel file: {e}") from e

    return clean_dataframe(raw_df, source_label="Excel", job_id=job_id)  # ← pass it

"""
scoring/scoring_engine.py
──────────────────────────
Combines Stage 1 + 2 + 3 results into a single 0–100 score. (HARDENED edition)

The LLM is the scoring judge; if it fails OR returns malformed output, a
DETERMINISTIC fallback computes the score arithmetically so the pipeline ALWAYS
produces a valid result.

ICP GATE: a lead with any MAJOR DISQUALIFIER from Stage 1 (e.g.
outside_target_geography, non_target_industry, missing_valid_contact_phone)
is always forced to "Low" priority — even if its weighted score is over 50.

Design principle: neither the LLM path NOR the fallback path may crash — every
field is coerced, every stage result is accessed defensively (they may be None).

Import:
    from app.scoring.scoring_engine import score_lead
    result = score_lead(lead_data, icp_result, web_result, contact_result)
"""

import json
import logging
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.config import SCORING_WEIGHTS, get_priority
from app.llm_engine import llm_json, SYSTEM_SCORER

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_REASON_CHARS = 1500
MAX_GROWTH_CHARS = 1000
MAX_OUTREACH_CHARS = 3000

# Fallback weights if config is somehow malformed.
_DEFAULT_WEIGHTS = {
    "icp_fit": 0.30, "decision_maker": 0.25, "data_accuracy": 0.25,
    "growth_signals": 0.15, "contact_quality": 0.05,
}


# ── Safe helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _s(value, default: str = "") -> str:
    if value is None:
        return default
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value.strip()
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return ", ".join(_s(v) for v in value if v is not None).strip()
    try:
        return str(value).strip()
    except Exception:
        return default


def _num(value, default: float = 0.0) -> float:
    """Coerce to float, never raising. Handles None, '85', '85%', '85/100'."""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return 1.0 if value else 0.0
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        try:
            return float(value)
        except Exception:
            return default
    if isinstance(value, str):
        m = re.search(r"-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?", value)
        if m:
            try:
                return float(m.group())
            except Exception:
                return default
    return default


def _clamp(v: float, lo: float = 0.0, hi: float = 100.0) -> float:
    return max(lo, min(hi, v))


def _get(obj, attr, default=None):
    """Defensive getattr — stage results may be None or unexpected types."""
    try:
        return getattr(obj, attr, default)
    except Exception:
        return default


def _weights() -> dict:
    w = SCORING_WEIGHTS if isinstance(SCORING_WEIGHTS, dict) else {}
    # Merge over defaults so a missing key can't KeyError later.
    return {**_DEFAULT_WEIGHTS, **{k: _num(v, _DEFAULT_WEIGHTS.get(k, 0))
                                   for k, v in w.items()}}


def _priority(total: float) -> str:
    try:
        p = get_priority(total)
        return _s(p) or "Low"
    except Exception:
        # Local fallback tiers if config's get_priority misbehaves.
        if total >= 80:
            return "High"
        if total >= 50:
            return "Medium"
        return "Low"


def _priority_with_icp_gate(total: float, icp_result) -> str:
    """Mandatory ICP failures always produce Low priority."""
    major_disqualifiers = _get(
        icp_result, "major_disqualifiers", []
    ) or []

    if major_disqualifiers:
        return "Low"

    return _priority(total)


# ── Result dataclass ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@dataclass
class ScoreResult:
    total_score:     float = 0.0
    icp_score:       float = 0.0
    authority_score: float = 0.0
    data_score:      float = 0.0
    growth_score:    float = 0.0
    contact_score:   float = 0.0
    priority:        str   = "Low"
    reasoning:       str   = ""
    growth_signals:  str   = ""
    outreach_intelligence: str = ""
    raw_output:      str   = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "total_score":     self.total_score,
            "icp_score":       self.icp_score,
            "authority_score": self.authority_score,
            "data_score":      self.data_score,
            "growth_score":    self.growth_score,
            "contact_score":   self.contact_score,
            "priority":        self.priority,
            "reasoning":       self.reasoning,
            "growth_signals":  self.growth_signals,
            "outreach_intelligence": self.outreach_intelligence,
        }


# ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def score_lead(lead_data, icp_result, web_result, contact_result,
               job_id=None, lead_id=None) -> ScoreResult:
    """
    Final scoring pass. Never raises. LLM path first; deterministic fallback on
    any failure or malformed output.
    """
    if not isinstance(lead_data, dict):
        lead_data = {}

    try:
        prompt = _build_prompt(lead_data, icp_result, web_result, contact_result)
        raw = llm_json(prompt, system=SYSTEM_SCORER, max_output_tokens=900,
                   stage="score", module="scoring_engine",
                   job_id=job_id, lead_id=lead_id)
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            logger.warning(f"scoring: LLM returned non-dict ({type(raw)}) — fallback")
            return _deterministic_fallback(icp_result, web_result, contact_result,
                                           "LLM non-dict output")
        result = _parse_response(raw, icp_result)
        try:
            result.raw_output = json.dumps(raw, default=str)
        except Exception:
            result.raw_output = "{}"
        logger.debug(
            f"Score [{_s(lead_data.get('Contact Name')) or '?'}] "
            f"total={result.total_score} priority={result.priority}"
        )
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(
            f"LLM scoring failed for '{_s(lead_data.get('Contact Name')) or '?'}': "
            f"{e} — using fallback"
        )
        return _deterministic_fallback(icp_result, web_result, contact_result, str(e))


# ── Prompt ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _build_prompt(lead_data, icp_result, web_result, contact_result) -> str:
    w = _weights()

    # Only include non-empty lead fields, capped
    lead_summary = "\n".join(
        f"  {k}: {_s(v)[:80]}"          # ← cap each field at 80 chars
        for k, v in lead_data.items()
        if _s(v) and k in (             # ← only fields scorer actually needs
            "Contact Name", "Job Title", "Company Name",
            "Industry", "Employee Count", "Annual Revenue",
            "Email Address", "Phone Number",
        )
    ) or "  (no data)"

    return f"""
Score this B2B lead 0-100 using the stage results below.

LEAD:
{lead_summary}

STAGE 1 — ICP:
  icp_match={_get(icp_result,'icp_match')} score={_num(_get(icp_result,'icp_score'))}/10
  matched={_get(icp_result,'matched_criteria',[])}
  mismatches={_get(icp_result,'mismatches',[])}
  disqualifiers={_get(icp_result,'major_disqualifiers',[])}
  dm_score={_num(_get(icp_result,'decision_maker_score'))}/10

STAGE 2 — WEB:
  company_ok={_get(web_result,'company_verified')}
  person_ok={_get(web_result,'person_verified')}
  industry_ok={_get(web_result,'industry_confirmed')}
  headcount_ok={_get(web_result,'employee_count_confirmed')}
  techs={len(_get(web_result,'technologies_found') or [])} found
  has_growth={bool(_get(web_result,'growth_signals') and 'none' not in str(_get(web_result,'growth_signals','')).lower())}
  company_summary={_s(_get(web_result,'company_summary'))[:400]}
  technologies={_s(_get(web_result,'technologies_found'))[:300]}
  growth_evidence={_s(_get(web_result,'growth_signals'))[:500]}

STAGE 3 — CONTACT:
  email_valid={_get(contact_result,'email_valid')}
  phone_valid={_get(contact_result,'phone_valid')}
  employed={_get(contact_result,'employment_confirmed')}
  quality={_num(_get(contact_result,'contact_quality_score'))}/10

WEIGHTS: icp={w['icp_fit']*100:.0f}% dm={w['decision_maker']*100:.0f}% data={w['data_accuracy']*100:.0f}% growth={w['growth_signals']*100:.0f}% contact={w['contact_quality']*100:.0f}%

Return ONLY this JSON:
{{
  "total_score": <float 0-100>,
  "icp_score": <float 0-100>,
  "authority_score": <float 0-100>,
  "data_score": <float 0-100>,
  "growth_score": <float 0-100>,
  "contact_score": <float 0-100>,
  "reasoning": "<2-3 sentence sales summary>",
  "growth_signals": "<1-2 sentence growth summary or 'None identified'>",
  "outreach_intelligence": {{
    "recommended_channel": "<email|phone|linkedin>",
    "best_angle": "<specific value proposition grounded in the evidence>",
    "opening_message": "<personalized, concise first-touch message; do not invent facts>",
    "talking_points": ["<point 1>", "<point 2>"],
    "objection_watchout": "<likely objection and how to handle it>"
  }}
}}""".strip()

# ── Response parser (type-safe) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
def _parse_response(raw: dict, icp_result=None) -> ScoreResult:
    # Accept the model's component scores, but compute the final score ourselves.
    # This prevents an LLM arithmetic error from being stored or displayed.
    icp_score       = _clamp(_num(raw.get("icp_score")))
    authority_score = _clamp(_num(raw.get("authority_score")))
    data_score      = _clamp(_num(raw.get("data_score")))
    growth_score    = _clamp(_num(raw.get("growth_score")))
    contact_score   = _clamp(_num(raw.get("contact_score")))

    w = _weights()
    total = round(
        icp_score * w["icp_fit"]
        + authority_score * w["decision_maker"]
        + data_score * w["data_accuracy"]
        + growth_score * w["growth_signals"]
        + contact_score * w["contact_quality"],
        1,
    )

    outreach = raw.get("outreach_intelligence")
    if isinstance(outreach, dict):
        outreach = json.dumps(outreach, ensure_ascii=False)

    return ScoreResult(
        total_score=total,
        icp_score=icp_score,
        authority_score=authority_score,
        data_score=data_score,
        growth_score=growth_score,
        contact_score=contact_score,
        priority=_priority_with_icp_gate(total, icp_result),
        reasoning=_s(raw.get("reasoning"))[:MAX_REASON_CHARS],
        growth_signals=_s(raw.get("growth_signals"))[:MAX_GROWTH_CHARS]
        or "None identified",
        outreach_intelligence=_s(outreach)[:MAX_OUTREACH_CHARS],
    )


# ── Deterministic fallback (no LLM) — also fully guarded ─────────────────────
def _deterministic_fallback(icp_result, web_result, contact_result, error_msg) -> ScoreResult:
    w = _weights()

    icp_s       = _clamp(_num(_get(icp_result, "icp_score")) * 10.0)
    authority_s = _clamp(_num(_get(icp_result, "decision_maker_score")) * 10.0)

    web_checks = sum(1 for f in (
        _get(web_result, "company_verified"),
        _get(web_result, "person_verified"),
        _get(web_result, "industry_confirmed"),
        _get(web_result, "employee_count_confirmed"),
    ) if bool(f))
    data_s = (web_checks / 4.0) * 100.0

    growth_raw = _s(_get(web_result, "growth_signals"))
    growth_s = 60.0 if growth_raw and "none" not in growth_raw.lower() else 10.0

    contact_s = _clamp(_num(_get(contact_result, "contact_quality_score")) * 10.0)

    total = _clamp(
        icp_s       * w["icp_fit"] +
        authority_s * w["decision_maker"] +
        data_s      * w["data_accuracy"] +
        growth_s    * w["growth_signals"] +
        contact_s   * w["contact_quality"]
    )
    total = round(total, 1)

    r = ScoreResult(
        total_score=     total,
        icp_score=       round(icp_s, 1),
        authority_score= round(authority_s, 1),
        data_score=      round(data_s, 1),
        growth_score=    round(growth_s, 1),
        contact_score=   round(contact_s, 1),
        priority=        _priority_with_icp_gate(total, icp_result),
        reasoning=       f"Fallback scoring used (LLM error: {_s(error_msg)[:200]})",
        growth_signals=  growth_raw or "None identified",
        outreach_intelligence=json.dumps({
            "recommended_channel": "email" if bool(_get(contact_result, "email_valid")) else "linkedin",
            "best_angle": "Lead with the verified ICP fit and a relevant business outcome.",
            "opening_message": "Introduce the relevant outcome, reference only verified company context, and ask for a short discovery call.",
            "talking_points": ["ICP-aligned business outcome", "Verified company context"],
            "objection_watchout": "Confirm current priorities before proposing a detailed solution.",
        }),
    )
    try:
        r.raw_output = json.dumps({"error": _s(error_msg), "fallback": True})
    except Exception:
        r.raw_output = "{}"
    return r

END FILE: backend/app/scoring/scoring_engine.py

"""
scraper/web_scraper.py
──────────────────────
Company website scraper for the RAG ingestion pipeline.

Adapted from a previous project's research module — LangSearch fallback and the
Django `settings` dependency have been REMOVED. Pure website scrape only:

    requests + BeautifulSoup → normalize URL → strip boilerplate → clean text.

Primary (and only) source: the company's own website. If the site is
unreachable, the URL is missing, or the scrape yields too little usable text,
we simply return whatever we have (possibly empty) and let the caller decide.

Single entry point the pipeline should call:

    from app.scraper.web_scraper import research_company
    result = research_company(company_name, website_url)
    # → {"text": str, "source": "scrape"|"none", "source_url": str, "reachable": bool}
"""

import logging
import re

import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

REQUEST_TIMEOUT = 8   # seconds — pipeline processes many leads, fail fast
USER_AGENT = (
    "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; LeadQualification/1.0; "
    "+research-bot for internal lead qualification)"
)
MIN_USEFUL_TEXT_LENGTH = 200   # below this, the scrape is "not useful enough"


# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _normalize_url(url: str) -> str:
    """Add https:// if the scheme is missing; trim whitespace."""
    url = (url or "").strip()
    if not url:
        return ""
    if not re.match(r"^https?://", url, re.IGNORECASE):
        url = f"https://{url}"
    return url


# ── Scrape ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def scrape_website(url: str) -> dict:
    """
    Fetch a company website and extract visible text, stripping common
    boilerplate (scripts, styles, nav, footer, noscript).

    Returns:
        {"text": str, "reachable": bool, "source": "scrape", "source_url": str}
    """
    normalized = _normalize_url(url)
    if not normalized:
        return {"text": "", "reachable": False, "source": "scrape", "source_url": ""}

    try:
        response = requests.get(
            normalized,
            headers={"User-Agent": USER_AGENT},
            timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
            allow_redirects=True,
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
    except requests.RequestException as exc:
        logger.info("scrape_website: %s unreachable (%s)", normalized, exc)
        return {"text": "", "reachable": False, "source": "scrape",
                "source_url": normalized}

    final_url = response.url or normalized

    # Prefer lxml; fall back to the stdlib parser if lxml isn't installed.
    try:
        soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, "lxml")
    except Exception:
        soup = BeautifulSoup(response.text, "html.parser")

    for tag in soup(["script", "style", "nav", "footer", "noscript"]):
        tag.decompose()

    text = soup.get_text(separator=" ", strip=True)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()

    return {"text": text, "reachable": True, "source": "scrape",
            "source_url": final_url}


# ── Orchestrator (single entry point) ─────────────────────────────────────────
def research_company(company_name: str, website_url: str) -> dict:
    """
    Scrape one company's website and return its cleaned text.

    (LangSearch fallback removed — website scrape is the only source now.)

    Returns:
        {"text": str, "source": "scrape"|"none", "source_url": str,
         "reachable": bool}
    """
    scrape_result = scrape_website(website_url)

    if scrape_result["reachable"] and \
            len(scrape_result["text"]) >= MIN_USEFUL_TEXT_LENGTH:
        return {
            "text":       scrape_result["text"],
            "source":     "scrape",
            "source_url": scrape_result["source_url"],
            "reachable":  True,
        }

    # Reachable but thin, or unreachable — return whatever text we managed to get.
    if scrape_result["text"]:
        logger.info(
            "research_company: thin scrape for %s (%d chars)",
            company_name or website_url, len(scrape_result["text"]),
        )
        return {
            "text":       scrape_result["text"],
            "source":     "scrape",
            "source_url": scrape_result["source_url"],
            "reachable":  scrape_result["reachable"],
        }

    logger.info("research_company: no usable text for %s",
                company_name or website_url)
    return {"text": "", "source": "none",
            "source_url": scrape_result["source_url"], "reachable": False}

END FILE: backend/app/scraper/web_scraper.py

FILE 26/84
PATH: backend/app/tasks/embed_task.py

"""
tasks/embed_task.py
───────────────────
Async ingestion of scraped website text into Qdrant. (HARDENED edition)

Flow:
  process_lead (after enrichment)
      → persist_and_dispatch_embed(db, lead, web_result, clog=clog)
            → research_company(...)            # SCRAPE + PARSE
            → archive raw text to Blob          (Layer A)
            → save clean text to CompanyScrape  (Layer B, SQL)
            → embed_company.delay(scrape.id)    # async
                  → chunk → embed → upsert to Qdrant (Layer C)

Design principle: RAG ingestion is a BEST-EFFORT side-feature. It must NEVER
crash the main lead pipeline, and its own async task must fail gracefully
(mark the row done, don't retry forever) rather than hang the queue.
"""

import logging
from datetime import datetime

from app.celery_app import celery
from app.database import db_session
from app.scraper.web_scraper import research_company
from app.utils.company_logger import IST
from app.vectorstore.embedder import chunk_text, embed
from app.vectorstore.qdrant_store import upsert_chunks
from app.models import CompanyScrape

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_NOT_FOUND_RETRIES = 5   # give the parent txn time to commit, then give up


# ── Safe helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _s(value, default: str = "") -> str:
    if value is None:
        return default
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return value.strip()
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return ", ".join(_s(v) for v in value if v is not None).strip()
    try:
        return str(value).strip()
    except Exception:
        return default


# ── Task: embed one company's scraped text ────────────────────────────────────
@celery.task(
    name="app.tasks.embed_task.embed_company",
    bind=True,
    max_retries=5,
    default_retry_delay=30,
    acks_late=True,
)
def embed_company(self, scrape_id: str):
    """Chunk → embed → upsert a single company's scraped text into Qdrant."""
    with db_session() as db:
        try:
            scrape = db.query(CompanyScrape).filter(CompanyScrape.id == scrape_id).first()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"embed_company: DB load failed for {scrape_id}: {e}")
            raise self.retry(exc=e, countdown=15)

        if not scrape:
            # Parent process_lead txn may not have committed yet — retry a few
            # times, then give up (don't retry forever on a genuinely missing row).
            if self.request.retries >= MAX_NOT_FOUND_RETRIES:
                logger.error(
                    f"CompanyScrape {scrape_id} still not found after "
                    f"{MAX_NOT_FOUND_RETRIES} retries — giving up"
                )
                return
            logger.warning(
                f"CompanyScrape {scrape_id} not found yet "
                f"(retry {self.request.retries + 1}/{MAX_NOT_FOUND_RETRIES})"
            )
            raise self.retry(countdown=30)

        company = _s(scrape.company_name) or "Unknown"

        # ── Chunk (guarded) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        try:
            chunks = chunk_text(_s(scrape.clean_text)) or []
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"chunk_text failed for '{company}' ({scrape_id}): {e}")
            chunks = []

        if not chunks:
            logger.info(f"No text to embed for '{company}'")
            _mark_done(db, scrape, count=0)
            return

        # ── Embed (guarded — retry, since API blips are transient) ───────────
        try:
            vectors = embed(chunks) or []
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(f"embed() failed for '{company}' ({scrape_id}): {exc}")
            raise self.retry(exc=exc)

        # Guard: vectors must align 1:1 with chunks, else Qdrant upsert corrupts.
        if len(vectors) != len(chunks):
            logger.warning(
                f"embed count mismatch for '{company}': "
                f"{len(vectors)} vectors vs {len(chunks)} chunks — truncating to min"
            )
            n = min(len(vectors), len(chunks))
            chunks, vectors = chunks[:n], vectors[:n]

        if not vectors:
            logger.warning(f"No vectors produced for '{company}' — marking done (0)")
            _mark_done(db, scrape, count=0)
            return

        # ── Upsert (guarded — retry on transient Qdrant issues) ──────────────
        try:
            written = upsert_chunks(
                job_id=scrape.job_id,
                lead_id=scrape.lead_id,
                company_name=company,
                chunks=chunks,
                vectors=vectors,
                website_url=_s(scrape.website_url),
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(f"upsert_chunks failed for '{company}' ({scrape_id}): {exc}")
            raise self.retry(exc=exc)

        _mark_done(db, scrape, count=written)
        logger.info(f"Embedded '{company}' (job={scrape.job_id}): {written} chunks")


def _mark_done(db, scrape, count: int) -> None:
    """Flag the scrape row as embedded. Never raises hard."""
    try:
        scrape.chunk_count = int(count or 0)
        scrape.embedded = 1
        db.flush()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"_mark_done failed for {getattr(scrape, 'id', '?')}: {e}")


# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
# Called from process_lead (after AI enrichment). Best-effort — never breaks
# the main pipeline.
#     persist_and_dispatch_embed(db, lead, web_result, clog=clog)
# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def persist_and_dispatch_embed(db, lead, web_result=None, clog=None) -> None:
    """
    SCRAPE + PARSE + STORE(A/B) + dispatch embedding for one company.
    Enriched fields (already on `lead`) are appended for richer chunks.
    """
    try:
        if lead is None:
            logger.warning("persist_and_dispatch_embed: lead is None — skipping")
            return

        company = _s(getattr(lead, "company_name", "")) or "Unknown"
        website = _s(getattr(lead, "company_website", ""))
        t_scrape = datetime.now(IST)

        # ── 1️⃣ + 2️⃣  SCRAPE + PARSE (guarded) ──────────────────────────────
        try:
            research = research_company(company, website)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"research_company failed for '{company}': {e}")
            research = {}
        if not isinstance(research, dict):
            research = {}

        scraped_text = _s(research.get("text"))
        source_url = _s(research.get("source_url")) or website

        # ── Merge enriched fields for richer chunks ──────────────────────────
        clean_text = "\n\n".join(b for b in [
            scraped_text,
            _s(getattr(lead, "company_description", "")),
            _s(getattr(lead, "technologies_used", "")),
        ] if b).strip()

        if not clean_text:
            logger.info(f"persist_and_dispatch_embed: no text for '{company}'")
            _safe_clog_scrape(clog, research, clean_text="", blob_path="", started=t_scrape)
            return

        # ── 3️⃣  STORE Layer A: raw scrape → Blob (best-effort) ──────────────
        blob_path = _archive_raw_to_blob(getattr(lead, "job_id", None),
                                         getattr(lead, "id", None), research)

        # ── Log the scrape (guarded) ─────────────────────────────────────────
        _safe_clog_scrape(clog, research, clean_text=clean_text,
                          blob_path=blob_path, started=t_scrape)

        # ── 3️⃣  STORE Layer B: clean text → SQL (own isolated session so it
        #         never poisons the parent process_lead transaction) ──────────
        scrape_id = None
        try:
            with db_session() as embed_db:
                clean_text_db = clean_text[:4000] if len(clean_text) > 4000 else clean_text
                scrape = CompanyScrape(
                    job_id=getattr(lead, "job_id", None),
                    lead_id=getattr(lead, "id", None),
                    company_name=company,
                    website_url=source_url,
                    clean_text=clean_text_db,
                    blob_path=blob_path,
                    embedded=0,
                )
                embed_db.add(scrape)
                embed_db.flush()
                scrape_id = scrape.id
            # session committed here — embed task can find the row immediately
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"CompanyScrape persist failed for '{company}': {e}")

        # ── Dispatch async embedding (guarded so a broker blip doesn't bubble)
        if scrape_id:
            try:
                embed_company.delay(scrape_id)
                logger.info(
                    f"Dispatched embed for '{company}' "
                    f"(job={getattr(lead, 'job_id', '?')}, source={research.get('source')})"
                )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"embed dispatch failed for '{company}': {e}")

    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(
            f"persist_and_dispatch_embed skipped for lead "
            f"{getattr(lead, 'id', '?')}: {e}"
        )

def _safe_clog_scrape(clog, research, clean_text, blob_path, started) -> None:
    """Call clog.scrape() without ever letting a logging error bubble up."""
    if not clog:
        return
    try:
        clog.scrape(research, clean_text=clean_text,
                    source="app/scraper/web_scraper.py",
                    blob_path=blob_path, started=started)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug(f"clog.scrape failed: {e}")


def _archive_raw_to_blob(job_id, lead_id, research: dict) -> str:
    """Store the raw scrape result in Blob; return blob path (or '')."""
    try:
        import json
        from azure.storage.blob import BlobServiceClient
        from app.config import settings

        payload = json.dumps(research, default=str)
        client = BlobServiceClient.from_connection_string(
            settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONNECTION_STRING
        )
        container = client.get_container_client(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONTAINER)
        blob_name = f"scraped/{job_id}/{lead_id}/raw.json"
        container.get_blob_client(blob_name).upload_blob(
            payload.encode("utf-8"), overwrite=True
        )
        return blob_name
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Blob archive failed for lead {lead_id}: {e}")
        return ""

END FILE: backend/app/tasks/embed_task.py

"""
tasks/import_task.py
────────────────────
Background file import. (NEW)

Keeps the /upload request instant regardless of file size or LLM
column-mapping latency by moving ALL heavy work off the request thread.

Flow:
  POST /upload  → store raw file to Blob, create Job(status="importing"),
                  return job_id IMMEDIATELY, dispatch import_file.delay(...)
  import_file   → download bytes → parse (+ LLM column map) → deduplicate →
                  bulk-insert leads → Job.status = "queued"  (ready for ICP)
                  on any failure → Job.status = "import_failed" + import_error

The frontend polls GET /jobs/{job_id}/status and watches for:
  "importing"     → still parsing/inserting (show a spinner)
  "queued"        → done; total_rows + duplicates_removed are populated
  "import_failed" → show job.import_error

Wire-in:
  1. Register in celery_app.py:
       include=["app.tasks.tasks", "app.tasks.embed_task", "app.tasks.import_task"]
  2. Dispatch from /upload:  import_file.delay(job_id, blob_name, suffix, filename)
"""

import logging
import uuid

from app.celery_app import celery
from app.database import db_session
from app.models import Job, Lead
from app.parsers.excel_parser import parse_excel
from app.parsers.csv_parser import parse_csv
from app.utils.deduplicator import deduplicate
from app.config import settings

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_NOT_FOUND_RETRIES = 5


# ── Safe cell reader (pandas fills missing values with NaN) ───────────────────
def _cell(row: dict, key: str) -> str:
    val = row.get(key, "")
    if val is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(val, float) and val != val:   # NaN is the only value != itself
        return ""
    return str(val).strip()


# ── Task ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@celery.task(
    name="app.tasks.import_task.import_file",
    bind=True,
    max_retries=5,
    default_retry_delay=15,
    acks_late=True,
)
def import_file(self, job_id: str, blob_name: str, suffix: str, filename: str):
    """Parse → map → dedup → bulk-insert. Marks the job queued or import_failed."""
    with db_session() as db:
        try:
            job = db.query(Job).filter(Job.id == job_id).first()
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"import_file: DB load failed for {job_id}: {e}")
            raise self.retry(exc=e, countdown=15)

        if not job:
            # Parent /upload transaction may not have committed yet — retry a few
            # times, then give up rather than looping forever.
            if self.request.retries >= MAX_NOT_FOUND_RETRIES:
                logger.error(f"import_file: job {job_id} not found after retries — giving up")
                return
            logger.warning(f"import_file: job {job_id} not found yet — retrying")
            raise self.retry(countdown=15)

        try:
            raw_bytes = _download_blob(blob_name)
            if not raw_bytes:
                _fail(db, job, "Could not read the uploaded file from storage.")
                return

            # ── Parse (this is where the LLM column-mapping call happens) ─────
            if suffix in (".xlsx", ".xls", ".xlsm"):
                df = parse_excel(raw_bytes, job_id=job_id)
            else:
                df = parse_csv(raw_bytes, job_id=job_id)

            # ── Deduplicate ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
            df, dedup_stats = deduplicate(df)
            if len(df) == 0:
                _fail(db, job, "File contains no valid leads after deduplication.")
                return

            # ── Bulk-insert leads (single round-trip, not 500 add() calls) ────
            records = df.to_dict("records")
            mappings = []
            for idx, row in enumerate(records):
                mappings.append({
                    "id":                   str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    "job_id":               job_id,
                    "row_index":            idx,
                    "contact_name":         _cell(row, "Contact Name"),
                    "job_title":            _cell(row, "Job Title"),
                    "contact_department":   _cell(row, "Contact Department"),
                    "contact_seniority":    _cell(row, "Contact Seniority"),
                    "linkedin_profile_url": _cell(row, "LinkedIn Profile URL"),
                    "email_address":        _cell(row, "Email Address"),
                    "phone_number":         _cell(row, "Phone Number"),
                    "direct_phone_number":  _cell(row, "Direct Phone Number"),
                    "contact_country":      _cell(row, "Contact Country"),
                    "contact_state_region": _cell(row, "Contact State/Region"),
                    "contact_city":         _cell(row, "Contact City"),
                    "company_name":         _cell(row, "Company Name"),
                    "company_website":      _cell(row, "Company Website"),
                    "company_linkedin_url": _cell(row, "Company LinkedIn URL"),
                    "industry":             _cell(row, "Industry"),
                    "sub_industry":         _cell(row, "Sub Industry"),
                    "employee_count":       _cell(row, "Employee Count"),
                    "annual_revenue":       _cell(row, "Annual Revenue"),
                    "company_description":  _cell(row, "Company Description"),
                    "technologies_used":    _cell(row, "Technologies Used"),
                    "company_country":      _cell(row, "Company Country"),
                    "company_state_region": _cell(row, "Company State/Region"),
                    "company_city":         _cell(row, "Company City"),
                    "company_hq_location":  _cell(row, "Company HQ Location"),
                    "hq_phone_number":      _cell(row, "HQ Phone Number"),
                    "crm_account_id":       _cell(row, "CRM Account ID"),
                    "crm_contact_id":       _cell(row, "CRM Contact ID"),
                    "lead_source":          _cell(row, "Lead Source"),
                    "notes":                _cell(row, "Notes"),
                    "processing_status":    "pending",
                })

            # Bound memory/parameter pressure while retaining executemany speed.
            for start in range(0, len(mappings), 2000):
                db.bulk_insert_mappings(Lead, mappings[start:start + 2000])

            # ── Mark ready for ICP ────────────────────────────────────────────
            job.total_rows = len(df)
            try:
                job.duplicates_removed = int(dedup_stats.get("duplicates_removed", 0))
            except Exception:
                job.duplicates_removed = 0
            job.import_error = None
            job.status = "queued"
            db.flush()

            logger.info(f"import_file: job {job_id} imported {len(df)} leads "
                        f"({job.duplicates_removed} duplicates removed)")

        except ValueError as e:
            # Parsing errors (bad file, unreadable) — user-facing, no retry.
            _fail(db, job, str(e))
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"import_file failed for {job_id}: {e}")
            _fail(db, job, f"Import failed: {e}")


# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _fail(db, job, message: str) -> None:
    try:
        job.status = "import_failed"
        job.import_error = (message or "Import failed")[:1000]
        db.flush()
        logger.warning(f"import_file: job {getattr(job,'id','?')} import_failed: {message}")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"_fail could not update job: {e}")


def _download_blob(blob_name: str) -> bytes | None:
    try:
        from azure.storage.blob import BlobServiceClient
        client = BlobServiceClient.from_connection_string(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONNECTION_STRING)
        container = client.get_container_client(settings.AZURE_BLOB_CONTAINER)
        return container.get_blob_client(blob_name).download_blob().readall()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"import_file: blob download failed for '{blob_name}': {e}")
        return None

END FILE: backend/app/tasks/import_task.py
