import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion';
import {
  MessageSquare, X, Send, Loader2, Sparkles, ExternalLink, Wrench,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { getChatHistory, sendChat } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { pushToast } from '@/components/ui/Toast';
import { safeArr } from '@/lib/format';
import type { ChatMessage, ChatSource } from '@/types';
import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';

export function ChatWidget({ jobId }: { jobId: string }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const { data: history = [], isLoading: historyLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['chat-history', jobId],
    queryFn: () => getChatHistory(jobId),
    enabled: open,
    placeholderData: (prev) => prev,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });

  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
  const [localMsgs, setLocalMsgs] = useState<ChatMessage[]>([]);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Merge server history with local optimistic messages
  const messages = [...safeArr(history), ...localMsgs];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (scrollRef.current) {
      scrollRef.current.scrollTop = scrollRef.current.scrollHeight;
    }
  }, [messages.length, open]);

  const handleSend = async () => {
    const text = input.trim();
    if (!text || sending) return;
    setInput('');
    setSending(true);
    const optimistic: ChatMessage = { role: 'user', content: text };
    setLocalMsgs((prev) => [...prev, optimistic]);

    try {
      const res = await sendChat(jobId, text);

      setLocalMsgs((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          role: 'assistant',
          content: res.answer,
          ...(res.sources?.length ? { sources: res.sources } : {}),
          ...(res.tool_used ? { tool_used: res.tool_used } : {}),
        } as ChatMessage & { sources?: ChatSource[]; tool_used?: string },
      ]);

      // Server history now contains this conversation.
      // Fetch it, then remove the temporary local copies.
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['chat-history', jobId] });
      setLocalMsgs([]);
    } catch {
      setLocalMsgs((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        { role: 'assistant', content: 'Sorry, I could not process that request. Please try again.' },
      ]);
      pushToast('Chat request failed', 'error');
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  };

  const handleKey = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSend();
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Toggle button */}
      <motion.button
        whileHover={{ scale: 1.05 }}
        whileTap={{ scale: 0.95 }}
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-40 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-gradient shadow-brand text-white"
        aria-label="Toggle chat"
      >
        {open ? <X className="h-6 w-6" /> : <MessageSquare className="h-6 w-6" />}
      </motion.button>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {open && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20, scale: 0.95 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 20, scale: 0.95 }}
            transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 300, damping: 26 }}
            className="fixed bottom-24 right-6 z-40 flex flex-col w-[calc(100vw-3rem)] sm:w-96 h-[32rem] max-h-[70vh] rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-card-hover ring-1 ring-slate-200 overflow-hidden"
          >
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-brand-gradient px-4 py-3 text-white">
              <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5" />
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold">Lead Assistant</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-white/70">Ask about this session's companies</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Messages */}
            <div ref={scrollRef} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 space-y-3 bg-slate-50/40">
              {historyLoading ? (
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center py-8">
                  <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-400 animate-spin" />
                </div>
              ) : messages.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 text-center">
                  <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-100 text-brand-400 mb-3">
                    <MessageSquare className="h-6 w-6" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">No messages yet</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-[220px]">
                    Ask me anything about this session's companies, scores, or growth signals…
                  </p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                messages.map((msg, i) => <MessageBubble key={i} msg={msg} />)
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Input */}
            <div className="border-t border-slate-100 p-3 bg-white">
              <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
                <textarea
                  value={input}
                  onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={handleKey}
                  placeholder="Type a message…"
                  rows={1}
                  className="flex-1 resize-none rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-brand-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100 max-h-24"
                />
                <button
                  onClick={handleSend}
                  disabled={!input.trim() || sending}
                  className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-600 text-white hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-40 transition-colors shrink-0"
                  aria-label="Send"
                >
                  {sending ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </>
  );
}

function MessageBubble({ msg }: { msg: ChatMessage & { sources?: ChatSource[]; tool_used?: string } }) {
  const isUser = msg.role === 'user';
  const sources = safeArr(msg.sources);
  const toolLabel = msg.tool_used
    ? msg.tool_used === 'sql_lookup' ? 'via database'
    : msg.tool_used === 'semantic_search' ? 'via web research'
    : `via ${msg.tool_used}`
    : null;

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      className={`flex ${isUser ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}
    >
      <div className={`max-w-[85%] ${isUser ? '' : 'w-full'}`}>
        <div
          className={`rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm leading-relaxed ${
            isUser
              ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white rounded-br-md'
              : 'bg-white text-slate-700 ring-1 ring-slate-100 rounded-bl-md'
          }`}
        >
                    {isUser ? (
            msg.content || '—'
          ) : (
            <div className="prose prose-sm max-w-none prose-slate
                            prose-p:my-1 prose-ol:my-1 prose-ul:my-1 prose-li:my-0.5
                            prose-a:text-sky-600">
              <ReactMarkdown>{msg.content || '—'}</ReactMarkdown>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Tool tag */}
        {toolLabel && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400">
            <Wrench className="h-3 w-3" /> {toolLabel}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Sources */}
        {sources.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mt-2">
            {sources.map((s, i) => (
              <a
                key={i}
                href={s.website_url?.startsWith('http') ? s.website_url : `https://${s.website_url}`}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-sky-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-sky-700 ring-1 ring-sky-200 hover:bg-sky-100 transition-colors"
              >
                {s.company_name}
                <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3" />
              </a>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

import {
  Building2, User, Mail, Award, Lightbulb, CheckSquare, Link2, Cpu, Send,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  Radar, RadarChart, PolarGrid, PolarAngleAxis, PolarRadiusAxis,
  ResponsiveContainer,
} from 'recharts';
import { Modal } from '@/components/ui/Modal';
import { Badge, PriorityBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { dash, num, safeArr, numVal } from '@/lib/format';
import type { LeadResult } from '@/types';

export function LeadDetailDrawer({ lead, open, onClose }: {
  lead: LeadResult | null;
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} size="xl" title="Lead Detail">
      {lead && <LeadDetailContent lead={lead} />}
    </Modal>
  );
}

function LeadDetailContent({ lead }: { lead: LeadResult }) {
  const outreach = parseOutreach(lead.outreach_intelligence);
  const radarData = [
    { metric: 'ICP',      value: numVal(lead.icp_score) },
    { metric: 'Authority',value: numVal(lead.authority_score) },
    { metric: 'Data',     value: numVal(lead.data_score) },
    { metric: 'Growth',   value: numVal(lead.growth_score) },
    { metric: 'Contact',  value: numVal(lead.contact_score) },
  ];
  const hasScores = radarData.some((d) => d.value > 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Top: company + final score */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-100 text-brand-600 shrink-0">
            <Building2 className="h-6 w-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-800">{dash(lead.company_name)}</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{dash(lead.job_title)}</p>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2">
              <PriorityBadge priority={lead.priority} />
              {lead.validation_status && <Badge color={lead.validation_status === 'Passed' ? 'success' : 'warning'}>{lead.validation_status}</Badge>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center bg-brand-50 rounded-2xl px-6 py-3 ring-1 ring-brand-100">
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Final Score</span>
          <span className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-700 mt-1">{num(lead.final_score)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Company block */}
      <Section icon={<Building2 className="h-4 w-4" />} title="Company">
        {lead.company_description ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{lead.company_description}</p>
        ) : (
          <Empty text="No company description available" />
        )}
        {lead.technologies_used && (
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-start gap-2">
            <Cpu className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Technologies</span>
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 mt-0.5">{lead.technologies_used}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {/* Source URLs */}
        <div className="mt-3">
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1.5"><Link2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />Sources</span>
          {safeArr(lead.source_urls).length > 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {lead.source_urls.map((url, i) => (
                <a
                  key={i}
                  href={url.startsWith('http') ? url : `https://${url}`}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-sky-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-sky-700 ring-1 ring-sky-200 hover:bg-sky-100 transition-colors"
                >
                  <Link2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                  {url.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '').slice(0, 40)}
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">No sources</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </Section>

      {/* Contact block */}
      <Section icon={<User className="h-4 w-4" />} title="Contact">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
          <InfoRow label="Name" value={dash(lead.contact_name)} />
          <InfoRow label="Job Title" value={dash(lead.job_title)} />
          <InfoRow label="Email" value={lead.email_address ?? '—'} icon={Mail} link={lead.email_address ? `mailto:${lead.email_address}` : undefined} />
        </div>
      </Section>

      {/* Scoring block */}
      <Section icon={<Award className="h-4 w-4" />} title="Scoring Breakdown">
        {hasScores ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 items-center">
            <div className="h-[200px]">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <RadarChart data={radarData}>
                  <PolarGrid stroke="#e2e8f0" />
                  <PolarAngleAxis dataKey="metric" tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }} />
                  <PolarRadiusAxis domain={[0, 100]} tick={{ fontSize: 9, fill: '#94a3b8' }} />
                  <Radar dataKey="value" stroke="#7c3aed" fill="#7c3aed" fillOpacity={0.25} strokeWidth={2} />
                </RadarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <ScoreRow label="ICP Score"       value={lead.icp_score} />
              <ScoreRow label="Authority Score" value={lead.authority_score} />
              <ScoreRow label="Data Score"      value={lead.data_score} />
              <ScoreRow label="Growth Score"    value={lead.growth_score} />
              <ScoreRow label="Contact Score"   value={lead.contact_score} />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Empty text="No scoring data available" />
        )}
      </Section>

      {/* Reasoning */}
      {lead.reasoning && (
        <Section icon={<Lightbulb className="h-4 w-4" />} title="AI Reasoning">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{lead.reasoning}</p>
        </Section>
      )}

      {/* Growth signals */}
      {lead.growth_signals && (
        <Section icon={<CheckSquare className="h-4 w-4" />} title="Growth Signals">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{lead.growth_signals}</p>
        </Section>
      )}

      {/* Actionable, evidence-grounded next best outreach */}
      {lead.outreach_intelligence && (
        <Section icon={<Send className="h-4 w-4" />} title="Outreach Intelligence">
          {outreach ? (
            <div className="space-y-3 text-sm">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">Best channel</span>
                <Badge color="brand">{outreach.recommended_channel || 'Not specified'}</Badge>
              </div>
              <InfoBlock label="Best angle" value={outreach.best_angle} />
              <InfoBlock label="Suggested opener" value={outreach.opening_message} quote />
              {outreach.talking_points.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Talking points</p>
                  <ul className="list-disc pl-5 space-y-1 text-slate-600">
                    {outreach.talking_points.map((point, index) => <li key={index}>{point}</li>)}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}
              <InfoBlock label="Objection watchout" value={outreach.objection_watchout} />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 whitespace-pre-wrap">{lead.outreach_intelligence}</p>
          )}
        </Section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

type Outreach = {
  recommended_channel: string;
  best_angle: string;
  opening_message: string;
  talking_points: string[];
  objection_watchout: string;
};

function parseOutreach(value: string | null): Outreach | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  try {
    const data = JSON.parse(value) as Partial<Outreach>;
    return {
      recommended_channel: String(data.recommended_channel ?? ''),
      best_angle: String(data.best_angle ?? ''),
      opening_message: String(data.opening_message ?? ''),
      talking_points: Array.isArray(data.talking_points) ? data.talking_points.map(String) : [],
      objection_watchout: String(data.objection_watchout ?? ''),
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function InfoBlock({ label, value, quote = false }: { label: string; value: string; quote?: boolean }) {
  if (!value) return null;
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">{label}</p>
      <p className={`leading-relaxed ${quote ? 'rounded-lg bg-white p-3 italic text-slate-700 ring-1 ring-slate-200' : 'text-slate-600'}`}>{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function Section({ icon, title, children }: { icon: React.ReactNode; title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50/60 ring-1 ring-slate-100 p-4">
      <h4 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-3">
        <span className="text-brand-500">{icon}</span>
        {title}
      </h4>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function InfoRow({ label, value, icon: Icon, link }: { label: string; value: string; icon?: typeof Mail; link?: string }) {
  const content = link ? (
    <a href={link} className="text-brand-600 hover:underline">{value}</a>
  ) : (
    <span className="text-slate-700">{value}</span>
  );
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
      {Icon && <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />}
      <div>
        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{label}: </span>
        {content}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ScoreRow({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number | null | undefined }) {
  const v = numVal(value);
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
      <span className="text-sm text-slate-600">{label}</span>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="w-24 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-200 overflow-hidden">
          <div className="h-full bg-brand-400 rounded-full" style={{ width: `${v}%` }} />
        </div>
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 tabular-nums w-10 text-right">{num(value)}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Empty({ text }: { text: string }) {
  return <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">{text}</p>;
}

import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { isLoggedIn } from '@/lib/auth';

export function ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  if (!isLoggedIn()) return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
}

import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Sparkles, LogOut, Activity, LayoutDashboard, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { getHealth } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { clearAuth, getUser } from '@/lib/auth';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/Toast';

export function TopBar() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const username = getUser();

  const { data: health } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['health'],
    queryFn: getHealth,
    refetchInterval: 30_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
    retry: 1,
  });

  const healthy = health?.status === 'ok' || health?.status === 'healthy';

  const handleLogout = () => {
    clearAuth();
    queryClient.clear();

    toast('Signed out successfully', 'info');
    window.location.replace('/login');
  };

  const navLink = (to: string, label: string, icon: React.ReactNode) => {
    const active = location.pathname === to || (to === '/dashboard' && location.pathname.startsWith('/jobs'));
    return (
      <Link
        to={to}
        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
          active ? 'bg-brand-100 text-brand-700' : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700'
        }`}
      >
        {icon}
        {label}
      </Link>
    );
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-slate-200/70 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-8">
          <Link to="/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-gradient shadow-brand">
              <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
            </div>
            <span className="hidden sm:block text-lg font-bold text-slate-800">LeadQual<span className="text-brand-600">AI</span></span>
          </Link>
          <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-1">
            {navLink('/dashboard', 'Dashboard', <LayoutDashboard className="h-4 w-4" />)}
            {navLink('/upload', 'Upload', <Upload className="h-4 w-4" />)}
          </nav>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          {/* Health pill */}
          <div
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ring-1 ring-inset ${
              healthy
                ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 ring-emerald-200'
                : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 ring-rose-200'
            }`}
            title={healthy ? 'System healthy' : 'System unreachable'}
          >
            <Activity className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${healthy ? '' : 'animate-pulse'}`} />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">System {healthy ? 'healthy' : 'unreachable'}</span>
            <span className="sm:hidden">{healthy ? 'OK' : 'Down'}</span>
          </div>

          {username && (
            <span className="hidden sm:block text-sm text-slate-500">
              <span className="text-slate-400">Hi,</span> <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{username}</span>
            </span>
          )}

          <button
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Logout"
          >
            <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Logout</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { JobStatus, Priority } from '@/types';

export function Badge({ children, color = 'neutral', className = '' }: {
  children: ReactNode;
  color?: 'neutral' | 'brand' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'info';
  className?: string;
}) {
  const colors = {
    neutral: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 ring-slate-200',
    brand:   'bg-brand-100 text-brand-700 ring-brand-200',
    success: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 ring-emerald-200',
    warning: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 ring-amber-200',
    danger:  'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 ring-rose-200',
    info:    'bg-sky-100 text-sky-700 ring-sky-200',
  };
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ring-1 ring-inset ${colors[color]} ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

export function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: JobStatus | null | undefined }) {
  if (!status) return <Badge>—</Badge>;

  const map: Record<JobStatus, { color: Parameters<typeof Badge>[0]['color']; label: string; pulse?: boolean }> = {
    queued:        { color: 'neutral', label: 'Queued' },
    running:       { color: 'info',    label: 'Running',        pulse: true },
    completed:     { color: 'success', label: 'Completed' },
    failed:        { color: 'danger',  label: 'Failed' },
    importing:     { color: 'warning', label: 'Importing',      pulse: true },
    import_failed: { color: 'danger',  label: 'Import Failed' },
  };

  const cfg = map[status] ?? { color: 'neutral' as const, label: status };

  return (
    <Badge color={cfg.color}>
      {cfg.pulse && <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-sky-500 animate-pulse" />}
      {cfg.label}
    </Badge>
  );
}

export function PriorityBadge({ priority }: { priority: Priority | null | undefined }) {
  if (!priority) return <Badge>—</Badge>;
  const map: Record<Priority, { color: Parameters<typeof Badge>[0]['color']; label: string }> = {
    High:   { color: 'danger',  label: 'High' },
    Medium: { color: 'warning', label: 'Medium' },
    Low:    { color: 'info',    label: 'Low' },
  };
  const cfg = map[priority];
  return <Badge color={cfg.color}>{cfg.label}</Badge>;
}

export function ScoreChip({ score }: { score: number | null | undefined }) {
  if (score === null || score === undefined || Number.isNaN(score)) {
    return <span className="text-slate-400">—</span>;
  }
  const n = Number(score);
  const color = n >= 75 ? 'text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 ring-emerald-200'
              : n >= 50 ? 'text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 ring-amber-200'
              : 'text-rose-600 bg-rose-50 ring-rose-200';
  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold ring-1 ring-inset ${color}`}>
      {n.toFixed(1)}
    </span>
  );
}

END FILE: frontend/src/components/ui/Badge.tsx

import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from 'react';

type Variant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'ghost' | 'danger' | 'outline';
type Size = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';

interface ButtonProps extends ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: Variant;
  size?: Size;
  loading?: boolean;
  icon?: ReactNode;
}

const variants: Record<Variant, string> = {
  primary:   'bg-gradient-to-r from-accent-400 to-accent-500 text-white hover:from-accent-500 hover:to-accent-600 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md font-semibold',
  secondary: 'bg-brand-600 text-white hover:bg-brand-700 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md font-semibold',
  ghost:     'text-slate-600 hover:bg-brand-50 hover:text-brand-700 font-medium',
  danger:    'bg-rose-500 text-white hover:bg-rose-600 shadow-sm font-semibold',
  outline:   'border border-brand-200 text-brand-700 hover:bg-brand-50 bg-white font-semibold',
};

const sizes: Record<Size, string> = {
  sm: 'h-8 px-3 text-xs rounded-lg gap-1.5',
  md: 'h-10 px-4 text-sm rounded-xl gap-2',
  lg: 'h-12 px-6 text-base rounded-xl gap-2',
};

export function Button({
  variant = 'primary',
  size = 'md',
  loading = false,
  icon,
  children,
  className = '',
  disabled,
  ...rest
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-200 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-400 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 ${variants[variant]} ${sizes[size]} ${className}`}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      {...rest}
    >
      {loading ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : icon}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

interface ModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl';
}

const sizeMap = {
  sm: 'max-w-md',
  md: 'max-w-lg',
  lg: 'max-w-2xl',
  xl: 'max-w-4xl',
};

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, children, size = 'md' }: ModalProps) {
  return (
    <AnimatePresence>
      {open && (
        <motion.div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
        >
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-950/40 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose} />
          <motion.div
            className={`relative w-full ${sizeMap[size]} rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-card-hover ring-1 ring-slate-200 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col`}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 20 }}
            transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 300, damping: 26 }}
          >
            {title && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800">{title}</h2>
                <button
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600 transition-colors"
                  aria-label="Close"
                >
                  <X className="h-5 w-5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="overflow-y-auto px-6 py-5">{children}</div>
          </motion.div>
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </AnimatePresence>
  );
}

PATH: frontend/src/components/ui/Toast.tsx

/* eslint-disable react-refresh/only-export-components -- provider and hook intentionally share this small module */
import { createContext, useContext, useCallback, useState, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, AlertTriangle, Info, X } from 'lucide-react';

type ToastType = 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';

interface Toast {
  id: number;
  type: ToastType;
  message: string;
}

interface ToastContextValue {
  toast: (message: string, type?: ToastType) => void;
}

const ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextValue | null>(null);

let toastId = 0;
const listeners = new Set<(t: Toast) => void>();

export const pushToast = (message: string, type: ToastType = 'info') => {
  const t = { id: ++toastId, type, message };
  listeners.forEach((l) => l(t));
};

export const useToast = (): ToastContextValue => {
  const ctx = useContext(ToastContext);
  return ctx ?? { toast: (m, t = 'info') => pushToast(m, t) };
};

const styles: Record<ToastType, { icon: typeof Info; ring: string; iconColor: string }> = {
  success: { icon: CheckCircle2, ring: 'ring-emerald-200', iconColor: 'text-emerald-500' },
  error:   { icon: XCircle,      ring: 'ring-rose-200',    iconColor: 'text-rose-500' },
  warning: { icon: AlertTriangle, ring: 'ring-amber-200',  iconColor: 'text-amber-500' },
  info:    { icon: Info,          ring: 'ring-brand-200',  iconColor: 'text-brand-500' },
};

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([]);

  const remove = useCallback((id: number) => setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const listener = (t: Toast) => {
      setToasts((prev) => [...prev, t]);
      setTimeout(() => remove(t.id), 5000);
    };
    listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { listeners.delete(listener); };
  }, [remove]);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ toast: (m, t = 'info') => pushToast(m, t) }}>
      {children}
      <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-[100] flex flex-col gap-3 pointer-events-none">
        {toasts.map((t) => {
          const s = styles[t.type];
          const Icon = s.icon;
          return (
            <div
              key={t.id}
              className={`pointer-events-auto flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl bg-white shadow-card-hover ring-1 ${s.ring} px-4 py-3 max-w-sm animate-slide-up`}
            >
              <Icon className={`h-5 w-5 shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${s.iconColor}`} />
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 flex-1 leading-snug">{t.message}</p>
              <button onClick={() => remove(t.id)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition-colors">
                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

FILE 68/84
PATH: frontend/src/hooks/useDashboardStats.ts

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { getDashboardStats } from '@/api/endpoints';

export interface DashboardStats {
  totalJobs: number;
  completedJobs: number;
  activeJobs: number;
  failedJobs: number;
  totalLeads: number;
  highPriority: number;
  mediumPriority: number;
  lowPriority: number;
  avgFinalScore: number;
}

const EMPTY: DashboardStats = {
  totalJobs: 0, completedJobs: 0, activeJobs: 0, failedJobs: 0,
  totalLeads: 0, highPriority: 0, mediumPriority: 0, lowPriority: 0,
  avgFinalScore: 0,
};

export function useDashboardStats(): { stats: DashboardStats; isLoading: boolean } {
  const { data, isLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['dashboard-stats'],
    queryFn: getDashboardStats,
    refetchInterval: 10_000,
    placeholderData: (prev) => prev,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });

  const stats: DashboardStats = data ? {
    totalJobs: data.total_jobs,
    completedJobs: data.completed_jobs,
    activeJobs: data.active_jobs,
    failedJobs: data.failed_jobs,
    totalLeads: data.total_leads,
    highPriority: data.high_priority,
    mediumPriority: data.medium_priority,
    lowPriority: data.low_priority,
    avgFinalScore: data.avg_final_score,
  } : EMPTY;
  return { stats, isLoading };
}

import axios from 'axios';

const BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';

export const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: BASE_URL,
  timeout: 60_000,
});

// Attach Bearer token to every request
apiClient.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }
  return config;
});

// On 401 clear storage and redirect to login
apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (res) => res,
  (err) => {
    if (err.response?.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('token');
      localStorage.removeItem('username');
      window.location.href = '/login';
    }
    return Promise.reject(err);
  },
);

export const getToken  = (): string | null => localStorage.getItem('token');
export const getUser   = (): string | null => localStorage.getItem('username');

export const setAuth = (token: string, username: string): void => {
  localStorage.setItem('token', token);
  localStorage.setItem('username', username);
};

export const clearAuth = (): void => {
  localStorage.removeItem('token');
  localStorage.removeItem('username');
};

export const isLoggedIn = (): boolean => !!getToken();

// ── Null-safe display helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const dash = <T,>(v: T | null | undefined): T | string => (v === null || v === undefined ? '—' : v);

export const num = (v: number | null | undefined, decimals = 1): string => {
  if (v === null || v === undefined || Number.isNaN(v)) return '—';
  return Number(v).toFixed(decimals);
};

export const safeArr = <T,>(v: T[] | null | undefined): T[] => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);

export const safeLen = <T,>(v: T[] | null | undefined): number => (Array.isArray(v) ? v.length : 0);

export const numVal = (v: number | null | undefined): number => (v === null || v === undefined || Number.isNaN(v) ? 0 : Number(v));

// ── Date formatting ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const fmtDate = (v: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!v) return '—';
  const d = new Date(v);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—';
  return d.toLocaleString(undefined, {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
};

export const fmtDateShort = (v: string | null | undefined): string => {
  if (!v) return '—';
  const d = new Date(v);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return '—';
  return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
};

// ── File size ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const fmtBytes = (bytes: number): string => {
  if (bytes === 0) return '0 B';
  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return `${parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1))} ${sizes[i]}`;
};

// ── File validation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const ALLOWED_EXT = ['.xlsx', '.xls', '.xlsm', '.csv'];

export const isValidFile = (name: string): boolean => {
  const lower = name.toLowerCase();
  return ALLOWED_EXT.some((ext) => lower.endsWith(ext));
};

END FILE: frontend/src/lib/format.ts

FILE 74/84
PATH: frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx

import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Briefcase, CheckCircle2, Loader2, Users, Flame, TrendingUp,
  Upload, FileSpreadsheet, FileText, Trash2, Eye,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { TopBar } from '@/components/TopBar';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button';
import { Badge, StatusBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { CardSkeleton, TableSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeleton';
import { useDashboardStats } from '@/hooks/useDashboardStats';
import { getJobs, deleteJob, downloadExport } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/Toast';
import { fmtDate, numVal, safeArr } from '@/lib/format';
import type { JobSummary } from '@/types';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';

export function DashboardPage() {
  const { stats, isLoading } = useDashboardStats();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const { data: jobs = [], isLoading: jobsLoading } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['jobs'],
    queryFn: getJobs,
    refetchInterval: 8_000,
    placeholderData: (prev) => prev,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });

  const handleDelete = async (jobId: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (!confirm('Delete this job and all its results?')) return;
    try {
      await deleteJob(jobId);
      toast('Job deleted', 'success');
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['jobs'] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['dashboard-stats'] });
    } catch {
      toast('Failed to delete job', 'error');
    }
  };

  const handleExport = async (jobId: string, format: 'excel' | 'csv', e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    try {
      await downloadExport(jobId, format);
      toast(`${format === 'excel' ? 'Excel' : 'CSV'} export downloaded`, 'success');
    } catch {
      toast(`Export failed`, 'error');
    }
  };

  const kpis = [
    { label: 'Total Jobs',      value: stats.totalJobs,         icon: Briefcase,    color: 'from-brand-500 to-brand-600' },
    { label: 'Jobs Completed',  value: stats.completedJobs,     icon: CheckCircle2, color: 'from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600' },
    { label: 'Running / Queued',value: stats.activeJobs,        icon: Loader2,      color: 'from-sky-500 to-sky-600', spin: stats.activeJobs > 0 },
    { label: 'Total Leads',     value: stats.totalLeads,        icon: Users,        color: 'from-indigo-500 to-indigo-600' },
    { label: 'High-Priority',   value: stats.highPriority,      icon: Flame,        color: 'from-rose-500 to-rose-600' },
    { label: 'Avg Final Score', value: stats.avgFinalScore.toFixed(1), icon: TrendingUp, color: 'from-accent-500 to-accent-600' },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-sky-gradient">
      <TopBar />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 space-y-8">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800">Dashboard</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-1">Overview of your lead qualification pipeline</p>
          </div>
          <Link to="/upload">
            <Button icon={<Upload className="h-4 w-4" />}>New Upload</Button>
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* KPI cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
          {isLoading
            ? Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => <CardSkeleton key={i} />)
            : kpis.map((k, i) => {
                const Icon = k.icon;
                return (
                  <motion.div
                    key={k.label}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 12 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: i * 0.05 }}
                  >
                    <Card hover className="p-5">
                      <div className={`inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${k.color} shadow-sm`}>
                        <Icon className={`h-5 w-5 text-white ${k.spin ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-800">{k.value}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{k.label}</p>
                    </Card>
                  </motion.div>
                );
              })}
        </div>

        {/* Charts */}


        {/* Jobs table */}
        <Card className="overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">Recent Jobs</h3>
            <Badge color="brand">{safeArr(jobs).length} total</Badge>
          </div>
          {jobsLoading ? (
            <div className="p-6"><TableSkeleton /></div>
          ) : jobs.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              icon={<Briefcase className="h-10 w-10" />}
              title="No jobs yet"
              subtitle="Upload a lead file to get started"
              action={<Link to="/upload"><Button icon={<Upload className="h-4 w-4" />}>Upload File</Button></Link>}
            />
          ) : (
            <JobsTable jobs={jobs} onDelete={handleDelete} onExport={handleExport} />
          )}
        </Card>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

function JobsTable({ jobs, onDelete, onExport }: {
  jobs: JobSummary[];
  onDelete: (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
  onExport: (id: string, fmt: 'excel' | 'csv', e: React.MouseEvent) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50">
            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-slate-600">File</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-slate-600">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-slate-600 min-w-[160px]">Progress</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-slate-600">Created</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold text-slate-600">Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-50">
          {jobs.map((job) => {
            const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, numVal(job.progress_pct)));
            return (
              <tr
                key={job.job_id}
                className="hover:bg-brand-50/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer group"
                onClick={() => (window.location.href = `/jobs/${job.job_id}`)}
              >
                <td className="px-6 py-3">
                  <div className="font-medium text-slate-800 truncate max-w-[200px]">{job.file_name ?? job.job_id}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">{job.file_type ?? '—'}</div>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3"><StatusBadge status={job.status} /></td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="flex-1 h-2 rounded-full bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all ${job.status === 'failed' ? 'bg-rose-400' : job.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-brand-400'}`}
                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums w-10 text-right">
                      {job.progress_pct != null ? `${pct.toFixed(0)}%` : '—'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    {job.processed_rows ?? 0} / {job.total_rows ?? 0} rows
                  </div>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap">{fmtDate(job.created_at)}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                    <Link to={`/jobs/${job.job_id}`} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={<Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}>View</Button>
                    </Link>
                    <button disabled={job.status !== 'completed'} onClick={(e) => onExport(job.job_id, 'excel', e)} title="Export Excel" className="rounded-lg p-2 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
                      <FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                    <button disabled={job.status !== 'completed'} onClick={(e) => onExport(job.job_id, 'csv', e)} title="Export CSV" className="rounded-lg p-2 text-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-50 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors">
                      <FileText className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                    <button onClick={(e) => onDelete(job.job_id, e)} title="Delete" className="rounded-lg p-2 text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50 transition-colors">
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export function EmptyState({ icon, title, subtitle, action }: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-16 px-4 text-center">
      <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-50 text-brand-300 mb-4">{icon}</div>
      <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-700">{title}</h3>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mt-1 mb-4">{subtitle}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

// re-export for use elsewhere

END FILE: frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx

==========================================================================================
FILE 75/84
PATH: frontend/src/pages/JobDetailPage.tsx

import { lazy, Suspense, useState, useEffect, useDeferredValue } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import {
  ArrowLeft, FileSpreadsheet, FileText, Trash2, RefreshCw, Search,
  ChevronUp, ChevronDown, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight,
  ChevronsLeft, ChevronsRight, Building2, Mail, Loader2, Target, Zap,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { TopBar } from '@/components/TopBar';
import { Card } from '@/components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/Button';
import { Badge, StatusBadge, PriorityBadge, ScoreChip } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { Input, Label, Textarea } from '@/components/ui/Input';
import { TableSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeleton';
import { EmptyState } from '@/pages/DashboardPage';
import { useToast } from '@/components/ui/Toast';
import { getJobStatus, getResultsPage, getJobs, deleteJob, downloadExport, startIcp } from '@/api/endpoints';
import { dash, fmtDate, safeArr } from '@/lib/format';
import type { LeadResult, Priority } from '@/types';

const LeadDetailDrawer = lazy(() => import('@/components/LeadDetailDrawer').then((module) => ({ default: module.LeadDetailDrawer })));
const ChatWidget = lazy(() => import('@/components/ChatWidget').then((module) => ({ default: module.ChatWidget })));

type SortKey = 'company_name' | 'contact_name' | 'email_address' | 'final_score' | 'priority';
type SortDir = 'asc' | 'desc';

// Server-side page size. Each "page" is one network request. 50 keeps requests
// small + fast while minimising round-trips. Next 2 pages are prefetched.
const PAGE_SIZE = 50;
const PREFETCH_AHEAD = 2;

export function JobDetailPage() {
  const { jobId = '' } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [priorityFilter, setPriorityFilter] = useState<Priority | 'all'>('all');
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>('final_score');
  const [sortDir, setSortDir] = useState<SortDir>('desc');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);                 // ← server page index (0-based)
  const [selectedLead, setSelectedLead] = useState<LeadResult | null>(null);
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [resumeIcp, setResumeIcp] = useState('');
  const [starting, setStarting] = useState(false);
  const deferredSearch = useDeferredValue(search.trim());

  // ── Job status (live poll while running) ──────────────────────────────────
  const { data: status } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['job-status', jobId],
    queryFn: () => getJobStatus(jobId),
    refetchInterval: (q) => {
      const s = q.state.data?.status;
      return s === 'completed' || s === 'failed' ? false : 3000;
    },
    placeholderData: (prev) => prev,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });

  const isCompleted = status?.status === 'completed';
  const isAwaitingIcp = status?.status === 'queued';
  const isRunning = status?.status === 'running' || status?.status === 'importing';

  // ── Current page of results (server-side, windowed) ───────────────────────
  const {
    data: pageResult,
    isLoading,
    isFetching,
  } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['job-results', jobId, page, PAGE_SIZE, deferredSearch, priorityFilter, sortKey, sortDir],
    queryFn: () => getResultsPage(jobId, page * PAGE_SIZE, PAGE_SIZE, {
      search: deferredSearch, priority: priorityFilter, sortBy: sortKey, sortDir,
    }),
    enabled: isCompleted,
    placeholderData: (prev) => prev,       // ← keep old rows visible → no flicker
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });

  const results: LeadResult[] = pageResult?.items ?? [];
  const total = pageResult?.total ?? 0;
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE));
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages - 1);

  // ── Prefetch the next N pages so "Next" feels instant ─────────────────────
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isCompleted) return;
    for (let i = 1; i <= PREFETCH_AHEAD; i++) {
      const next = currentPage + i;
      if (next >= totalPages) break;
      qc.prefetchQuery({
        queryKey: ['job-results', jobId, next, PAGE_SIZE, deferredSearch, priorityFilter, sortKey, sortDir],
        queryFn: () => getResultsPage(jobId, next * PAGE_SIZE, PAGE_SIZE, {
          search: deferredSearch, priority: priorityFilter, sortBy: sortKey, sortDir,
        }),
      });
    }
  }, [currentPage, totalPages, isCompleted, jobId, qc, deferredSearch, priorityFilter, sortKey, sortDir]);

  // ── Job meta (filename) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const { data: jobs = [] } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['jobs'],
    queryFn: getJobs,
    placeholderData: (prev) => prev,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
  });
  const fileName = status?.file_name ?? jobs.find((j) => j.job_id === jobId)?.file_name ?? null;

  const filtered = safeArr(results);

  const toggleSort = (key: SortKey) => {
    setPage(0);
    if (sortKey === key) {
      setSortDir((d) => (d === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'));
    } else {
      setSortKey(key);
      setSortDir('desc');
    }
  };

  const openLead = (lead: LeadResult) => {
    setSelectedLead(lead);
    setDrawerOpen(true);
  };

  const goToPage = (p: number) => setPage(Math.max(0, Math.min(totalPages - 1, p)));

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!confirm('Delete this job and all its results?')) return;
    try {
      await deleteJob(jobId);
      toast('Job deleted', 'success');
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['jobs'] });
      navigate('/dashboard');
    } catch {
      toast('Failed to delete job', 'error');
    }
  };

  const handleExport = async (format: 'excel' | 'csv') => {
    try {
      await downloadExport(jobId, format);
      toast(`${format === 'excel' ? 'Excel' : 'CSV'} downloaded`, 'success');
    } catch {
      toast('Export failed', 'error');
    }
  };

  const handleRefresh = () => {
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['job-results', jobId] });
  };

  const handleStartIcp = async () => {
    if (!resumeIcp.trim()) return;
    setStarting(true);
    try {
      await startIcp(jobId, resumeIcp.trim());
      toast('Processing started; you can safely return to the dashboard', 'success');
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['job-status', jobId] });
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['jobs'] });
    } catch {
      toast('Could not start processing. Refresh and try again.', 'error');
    } finally {
      setStarting(false);
    }
  };

  const sortIcon = (col: SortKey) =>
    sortKey === col ? (
      sortDir === 'asc' ? <ChevronUp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
    ) : null;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-sky-gradient">
      <TopBar />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 space-y-6">
        {/* Back link */}
        <Link to="/dashboard" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-brand-600 transition-colors">
          <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" /> Back to Dashboard
        </Link>

        {/* Job header */}
        <Card className="p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">{dash(fileName)}</h1>
                <StatusBadge status={status?.status} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                <span>Created: {fmtDate(status?.created_at)}</span>
                <span>Completed: {fmtDate(status?.completed_at)}</span>
                <span>Rows: {status?.total_rows ?? '—'}</span>
                <span>Processed: {status?.processed_rows ?? '—'}</span>
                {(status?.failed_rows ?? 0) > 0 && <span className="text-rose-600">Failed: {status?.failed_rows}</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" icon={<RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                onClick={handleRefresh}>
                Refresh
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={<FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-600" />}
                onClick={() => handleExport('excel')} disabled={!isCompleted}>
                Excel
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" icon={<FileText className="h-4 w-4 text-sky-600" />}
                onClick={() => handleExport('csv')} disabled={!isCompleted}>
                CSV
              </Button>
              <Button size="sm" variant="danger" icon={<Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />} onClick={handleDelete}>
                Delete
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Progress bar if running */}
          {isRunning && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500 mb-1.5">
                <span>Processing…</span>
                <span className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{(status?.progress_pct ?? 0).toFixed(0)}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="h-2.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
                <motion.div
                  className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-400 to-brand-600"
                  animate={{ width: `${status?.progress_pct ?? 0}%` }}
                  transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>

        {isAwaitingIcp && (
          <Card className="p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <Target className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-500" />
              <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800">Import ready — define the ICP</h2>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 mb-4">This job was imported but qualification has not started. Describe the target customer to resume it.</p>
            <Label htmlFor="resume-icp">Ideal Customer Profile</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="resume-icp"
              rows={4}
              value={resumeIcp}
              onChange={(event) => setResumeIcp(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="B2B companies in the target industry, geography, employee and revenue range…"
            />
            <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
              <Button onClick={handleStartIcp} disabled={!resumeIcp.trim() || starting} icon={starting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Zap className="h-4 w-4" />}>
                {starting ? 'Starting…' : 'Start Background Processing'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </Card>
        )}

        {/* Results table */}
        <Card className="overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
              Lead Results
              <Badge color="brand" className="ml-1">{total}</Badge>
              {isFetching && !isLoading && (
                <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-brand-400" aria-label="Loading page" />
              )}
            </h3>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
              <div className="relative">
                <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                <Input
                  placeholder="Search all leads…"
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(0); }}
                  className="pl-9 sm:w-56"
                />
              </div>
              <select
                value={priorityFilter}
                onChange={(e) => { setPriorityFilter(e.target.value as Priority | 'all'); setPage(0); }}
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-700 focus:border-brand-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-100"
              >
                <option value="all">All Priorities</option>
                <option value="High">High</option>
                <option value="Medium">Medium</option>
                <option value="Low">Low</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="p-6"><TableSkeleton /></div>
          ) : isRunning ? (
            <EmptyState
              icon={<RefreshCw className="h-10 w-10 animate-spin" />}
              title="Job still processing"
              subtitle="Results will appear here once the job completes"
            />
          ) : total === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              icon={<Building2 className="h-10 w-10" />}
              title="No results yet"
              subtitle="This job has no qualified leads"
            />
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              icon={<Building2 className="h-10 w-10" />}
              title="No matching leads on this page"
              subtitle="Try clearing the search or priority filter"
            />
          ) : (
            <>
              {/* Desktop table */}
              <div className="hidden md:block overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-sm">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50">
                      <Th onClick={() => toggleSort('company_name')}><span className="flex items-center gap-1">Company {sortIcon('company_name')}</span></Th>
                      <Th onClick={() => toggleSort('contact_name')}><span className="flex items-center gap-1">Contact {sortIcon('contact_name')}</span></Th>
                      <Th>Email</Th>
                      <Th onClick={() => toggleSort('final_score')}><span className="flex items-center gap-1">Score {sortIcon('final_score')}</span></Th>
                      <Th onClick={() => toggleSort('priority')}><span className="flex items-center gap-1">Priority {sortIcon('priority')}</span></Th>
                      <Th>Validation</Th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-50">
                    {filtered.map((lead) => (
                      <tr
                        key={lead.lead_id}
                        onClick={() => openLead(lead)}
                        className="hover:bg-brand-50/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <td className="px-6 py-3 font-medium text-slate-800 max-w-[200px] truncate">{dash(lead.company_name)}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-slate-600">{dash(lead.contact_name)}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-slate-600 max-w-[180px] truncate">{lead.email_address ?? '—'}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3"><ScoreChip score={lead.final_score} /></td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3"><PriorityBadge priority={lead.priority} /></td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          {lead.validation_status ? <Badge color={lead.validation_status === 'Passed' ? 'success' : 'warning'}>{lead.validation_status}</Badge> : <span className="text-slate-400">—</span>}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>

              {/* Mobile cards */}
              <div className="md:hidden divide-y divide-slate-50">
                {filtered.map((lead) => (
                  <div key={lead.lead_id} onClick={() => openLead(lead)} className="px-4 py-3 hover:bg-brand-50/40 cursor-pointer">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="font-medium text-slate-800 truncate">{dash(lead.company_name)}</span>
                      <ScoreChip score={lead.final_score} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      <span>{dash(lead.contact_name)}</span>
                      <PriorityBadge priority={lead.priority} />
                    </div>
                    {lead.email_address && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                        <Mail className="h-3 w-3" /> {lead.email_address}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Server-side pagination controls */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Showing{' '}
                  <span className="font-semibold tabular-nums">
                    {currentPage * PAGE_SIZE + 1}–{Math.min((currentPage + 1) * PAGE_SIZE, total)}
                  </span>{' '}
                  of <span className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{total}</span> leads
                </span>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <PagerBtn onClick={() => goToPage(0)} disabled={currentPage === 0} title="First page">
                    <ChevronsLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </PagerBtn>
                  <PagerBtn onClick={() => goToPage(currentPage - 1)} disabled={currentPage === 0} title="Previous">
                    <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </PagerBtn>

                  <span className="px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 tabular-nums">
                    Page {currentPage + 1} of {totalPages}
                  </span>

                  <PagerBtn onClick={() => goToPage(currentPage + 1)} disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1} title="Next">
                    <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </PagerBtn>
                  <PagerBtn onClick={() => goToPage(totalPages - 1)} disabled={currentPage >= totalPages - 1} title="Last page">
                    <ChevronsRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </PagerBtn>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </Card>
      </main>

      {/* Lead drawer */}
      {drawerOpen && (
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          <LeadDetailDrawer lead={selectedLead} open={drawerOpen} onClose={() => setDrawerOpen(false)} />
        </Suspense>
      )}

      {/* Chat widget (only when job completed) */}
      {isCompleted && <Suspense fallback={null}><ChatWidget jobId={jobId} /></Suspense>}
    </div>
  );
}

function Th({ children, onClick }: { children: React.ReactNode; onClick?: () => void }) {
  return (
    <th
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`px-6 py-3 text-left font-semibold text-slate-600 ${onClick ? 'cursor-pointer hover:text-brand-600 select-none' : ''} first:px-6 [&:not(:first-child)]:px-4`}
    >
      {children}
    </th>
  );
}

function PagerBtn({
  children, onClick, disabled, title,
}: { children: React.ReactNode; onClick: () => void; disabled?: boolean; title?: string }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      disabled={disabled}
      title={title}
      className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-brand-50 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

END FILE: frontend/src/pages/JobDetailPage.tsx

PATH: frontend/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx
